公司簡介 
中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅 ， 早期業
務專注於傳統砂輪 ， 後取得鑽石碟技術專利
授權，並因應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，
切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，
砂輪事業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。
2Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻
記、KTC、MKS、MGT) 25%、DBU 31%、
SBU 43%。

主要客戶：台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導
體、英特爾、欣興 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

中砂 (1560 TT) 
今天的我，會超越你想像的極限

永豐觀點 
早晚得千金。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價調升至 860 元：(1) 2Q26 因產品組合改善，毛利率提早達到 40%且下半年
繼續墊高，(2) 半導體大趨勢受惠股，除半導體/記憶體客戶持續擴產外，包含電晶
體結構改變、晶背供電、先進封裝均可帶動中砂營運成長。目標價隨2028 年 EPS
上修至 860 元(33x 2028 EPSF)。

ESG 評析 
中砂企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅：中砂成立於 1953 年，2005 年上市，目前資
本額約 14.6 億元，早期業務專注於傳統砂輪，後取得鑽石碟技術專利授權，並因
應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，砂輪事
業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。2Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻記、
KTC、MKS、MGT) 25%、DBU 31%、SBU 43%。中砂總部位於新北市鶯歌區，
砂輪、鑽石相關產品主要在鶯歌、樹林、湖口廠生產，而再生 /測試晶圓主要在新
竹竹北、竹南廠生產，另在日本有 1 座廠房、泰國有 2 座廠房。客戶涵蓋半導體/
記憶體製造商如台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導體、英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等。 
2Q26 優於研究部預期：中砂 2Q26 營收 24.9 億(+8.7%QoQ，+17.9%YoY)，所
有事業部需求均回升，包含(1) ABU 部分，AC 砂輪(傳統砂輪)受惠傳產機械、工具
機需求回升，而 BD 砂輪(鑽石/氮化硼砂輪)則隨 ABF 客戶需求增加而成長，營收
季增 16.3%，(2) DBU 受惠公司主要客戶在 2/1.6nm 製程出貨增加，營收季增
9.1%，(3) SBU 在產能提前建置後維持滿載，加上特殊晶圓出貨增加，營收季增
4.4%。毛利率因產能利用率提升、在客戶高階製程市占率較高而提升至 40.1%，
業外 0.6 億主要來自投資利益約 0.44 億，帶動稅後淨利達 5.2 億(+19.9%QoQ，
+96.5%YoY)，EPS 3.49 元。獲利表現優於預期，主因ABU、SBU 產品組合改善。 
獲利率成長具延續性：我們認為中砂後續獲利可穩步改善，主要來產品組合改善、
產能利用率提升，以及費用下降，包含(1) ABU 中高毛利的 BD 砂輪營收比重已由
1/3 提升至 50%，隨載板等高階應用需求增加，將持續推升 ABU 獲利率，公司亦
計劃增加 CVD 鑽石鍍膜所需設備因應。(2) SBU 新增產能以高階測試晶圓為主，
目前產能預期滿載至年底，2027 年則透過去瓶頸和增加高階設備持續擴產，有利
產品組合改善。(3) DBU 隨產能規模擴大，以及積極推動生產自動化，在人力增幅
低於產能增幅下，營運槓桿將逐步顯現。(4) 公司自 3Q24 起提列單季約 2,700 萬
的員工認股權費用，當時預期攤提約 10 季度，相關影響預期在 2H26-1H27 陸續
結束，亦有助於費用率下降。

### Page 3

3

受惠半導體大趨勢，中砂迎來結構性成長：除客戶擴產帶動出貨量增加以外，我們
認為製程複雜度提升也將持續增加中砂單位晶圓/封裝的產品用量，使公司具備優
於 WFE 市場的結構性成長機會，主要來自：(1) 先進製程放量：主要客戶 2/3nm
產能在 2026-2028 年持續擴張，公司鑽石碟市占率維持約 70-80%，直接受惠先
進製程投片增加；A14 預期 2028 年量產，目前研發階段已開始貢獻營收，隨A14
家族後續放量，營收貢獻可望顯著擴大；(2) 晶背供電提高 CMP 密度：隨部分先
進製程導入晶背供電，新增背面製程步驟將提高 CMP 製程道數，帶動單位晶圓鑽
石碟耗用量 ；(3) 高階測試/特殊晶圓需求增加： 邏輯與記憶體先進製程擴產將同步
推升高階測試晶圓需求 ， 使SBU 成長動能由傳統再生晶圓進一步延伸至高階應用 ；
(4) 先進封裝與 PLP：Foundry／IDM 持續擴充先進封裝產能將推升 BD 砂輪需
求，而 2028-2029 年面板級封裝逐步導入量產後，研磨面積放大亦有望進一步提
升單位產品用量。 
上調未來三年獲利預估 ：研究部預估中砂 2026 年全年營收 103.4 億(+27.0%YoY)，
毛利率 39.5%，稅後淨利 21.5 億(+58.5%YoY)，EPS 14.47 元(原估 13.65 元)。
2027 年營收 129.3 億 (+25.0%YoY) ，毛利率 41.5% ，稅後淨利 30.1 億
(+39.9%YoY)，EPS 20.26 元(原估 18.70 元)。2028 年營收 147.2 億(+13.9%YoY)，
毛利率 43.9%，稅後淨利 38.7 億(+28.6%YoY)，EPS 26.05 元(原估 22.46 元)。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

訪談速報 
巧新(1563) 
再生鋁與半導體雙引擎推升獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $80 
前次投資建議：2026.05.07 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價52

報告日期：2026.8.11 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/10 收盤價： 56.5 再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，給予投資評等「買
進」 
目前股價 13X 27(F)PER，考量：

(1) 公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，
營收較上季增加主要是因為下游客戶備貨較為積極；毛利
率 24.3%，QoQ+2.3ppts、YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用
比率顯著提升與產品優化；稅後淨利 7,119.7 萬元，
QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營
業費用受到運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33 元。 
(2) 受新車旺季與高端車款拉貨帶動，預估輪圈出貨 2026 年
逾 60 萬顆（下半年優於上半年），2027 年達 70 萬顆。
公司目標營業利益率回升至 20%；再生鋁採用率達 80%，
經 5個月生產與海運發酵後，將顯著拉升毛利率。 
(3) 屏東二廠預計 27Q2 投產，帶動總熔煉產能達10萬噸 （1/3
自用、2/3外銷代工） 。半導體原生鋁預計 26Q3完成認證，
下半年啟動小量出貨並於 2027 年放量。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 85.12 億元 ，YoY+7.7%； 毛利率29.1%，
YoY+4ppts；稅後淨利9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後EPS 
4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提
高以及半導體產品放量，因此投資評等上調至「買進」，6個
月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

訪談重點

公司為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠 
巧新成立於 1994 年，為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠，主要產品包含鍛造鋁圈、
鋁材、其他(主要為鍛造鋁懸吊系統元件 )。公司目前有三座生產基地，分別
是雲林場、屏東廠及德國廠，鍛造輪圈總年產能為90萬件(雲林廠 60 萬件、
屏東廠 30萬件)。

公司客戶包括全球知名車廠，Ferrari(法拉利)、Porsche(保時捷)、Mclaren(麥
拉倫)、Bently(賓利)、Aston Martin、賓士(含 AMG)、BMW(含 Rolls-Royce、
Mini)、Jaguar & Land Rover、Lexus、Lotus及電動卡車製造商 Workhouse
等。

客戶拉貨強勁抵銷匯損衝擊 
公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，營收較上季增加主
要是因為 下游客戶備貨較為積極 ；毛利率 24.3%， QoQ+2.3ppts、
YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用比率顯著提升與產品優化 ；稅後淨利 7,119.7
萬元，QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營業費用受到
運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33元。

半年輪圈出貨優於上半年，再生鋁採用率提升帶動毛利回升 
展望 26H2，鍛造輪圈受惠於車廠新車與改款車型通常於 10月開始組裝之旺
季需求，因此預計全年輪圈出貨量將超過 60 萬顆，且出貨下半年會優於上
半年。而 2027 年則是持續受惠於高端既有車款的熱門，帶動長期的需求以
及新車與改款的車型帶動下，預估 2027 年全年輪圈出貨量將達到 70萬顆

在毛利與成本表現方面，公司目標將整體營業利益率回升至 20%。由於產品
從場內生產製造（約 2個月）到海運運輸（約 3個月）至客戶端提貨認列存
在約 5 個月的時間差，故成本與毛利優化效益具延後發酵特性。公司目前獲
利優化之核心動能將來自再生鋁材的導入 ，由於再生鋁成本較原生鋁低約
10%至 15%，且截至 5、6月再生鋁佔鍛造輪圈原料採用率來到 80%，預計
將能有效提升毛利率。

### Page 3

作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
半導體驗證與產能擴張雙軌併進，帶動長期結構獲利改善 
公司持續拓展鋁材多元應用，目前於屏東設立的首座水平熔煉廠（產能 4 萬
噸）運作穩定，第二座垂直熔煉廠正在建置中，預計 26Q3 完成裝機與參數
調整後，並於 27Q2 投產，總熔煉產能規模規劃將達 10萬噸（約 1/3 自用、
2/3 外銷或代工）。在半導體材料領域，公司持續推進設備腔體與零件之送
樣與驗證作業，原生鋁材料預計 26Q3 完成認證並於下半年啟動少量出貨，
並於 2027 年放量。

再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，帶動營收獲利雙增 
預估 26Q3 營收 20.15 億元，QoQ+1.8%、YoY+18.4%；毛利率 25.6%，
QoQ+1.3ppts、YoY+9ppts，受惠於再生鋁採用率上升；稅後淨利 2.39 億元，
QoQ+235.8%、YoY+458.5%；稅後 EPS 1.06 元。

預估 2026年營收 79.02億元，YoY+13.3%；毛利率 25.1%，YoY+3.8ppts；
稅後淨利 6.7億元，YoY+110.4%；稅後 EPS 2.97元。

預估 2027年營收 85.12 億元，YoY+7.7%；毛利率 29.1%，YoY+4ppts；稅
後淨利 9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後 EPS 4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提高以及半導體產
品放量，因此投資評等上調至 「買進」，6個月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

2026 年業績指引上調，未來展望樂觀 
 2Q26 產品組合優化， 稅後EPS 16.55 元 ， 優於預期：亞德客-KY 2Q26
營收達 121.6 億元，+21.5%QoQ，+35.9%Y oY，係因電子產業維持雙
位數成長、汽車製造因持續增加市佔+20%Y oY，其中電池產業在中國
補貼政策下需求持續強勁，照明終端需求仍不佳，不過衰退幅 度已有
收斂跡象；稼動率部分：氣動元件產品維持100-110%高檔水準，線性
滑軌需求持續改善，稼動率由 1Q26 之 30%上升至 40%。獲利方面，
毛利率 49.7%，優於預期，係因 1)線性滑軌產能利用率回升；2)氣動
元件進入復甦週期 ； 營益率35.1%， 優於預期之34.6%， 業外損失600
萬人民幣，其中 2700 萬為固定資產處分損失(拆除舊廠房重建以提升
效率)，稅後 EPS 16.55 元，優於預期之 15.47 元。 
 2026 年產業穩健復甦，線軌稼動率提升改善毛利率表現，調整 2026
年 EPS 至 59.39 元：日本 6 月工具機訂單達 2,035 億日圓 ，+15%MoM，
+52.8%Y oY，累積+35.7%YoY，美國市場復甦累計+34.5%Y oY，中國
市場復甦累計+52.4%Y oY，歐洲市場復甦累計+25.4%YoY。展望後
市，管理層認為中國氣動元件市場進入復甦周期，預期全年有望雙位
數成長 ， 且公司成長性優於市場成長 ， 持續增加市場份額 ， 目標在2030
年前達到 35%市佔率；線軌部分，預期產能利用率由 1H26 的 30%提
升至年底的 50%時，線軌毛利率將達 30%，而在利用率進一步提升至 
80% 時，毛利率可望達 40%，總結前述，預估 2026 營收為 434.1 億
元，+26.5%Y oY，獲利部分，1)線軌稼動率逐步提升，有望改善產品
毛利率表現；2)中國在地化採購趨勢加速，帶動汽車/電池/太陽能市佔
率持續上升 ， 費用控管得宜 ， 不考慮一次性業外損益下 ， 調整稅後2026
年 EPS 至 59.39 元(原 56.47 元)，初估 2027 年 EPS 為 71.65 元。 
 產業谷底復甦，維持買進評等，目標價 1800 元：評價部分，當前公司
評價處 21x，過往評價區間處 21-33x 水準，處中性下緣，考量產業處
谷底復甦，故維持買進評等，目標價 1800 元(25x2027 EPS)

亞德客-KY 為氣動元件製造大廠 
 亞德客-KY 為氣動元件製造大廠 
亞德客-KY 為氣動元件製造大廠，主要競爭優勢來自於低階大量產品生產，取
得規模經濟後轉向中高階產品生產，為具有生產經濟規模與兼具品質之廠商。
主要廠區：寧波廠生產氣動元件部件整合與汽缸元件與大型線軌；廣東廠生產
氣動元件之氣閥；台灣台南廠生產較高端之汽缸與線軌。主要競爭對手為日商
SMC。2025 公司營收區域別：中國 94%、海外 4%、台灣 2%。2025 產業別
佔比：電子設備 26%、電池行業 20%、能源與照明設備 3%、通用機械 7%、
包裝機械 7%、汽車製造 9%、機床製造 5%、其他 23%。

2Q26 產品組合 優化，且內部效
率提升，稅後 EPS 16.55 元，優
於預期

2Q26 產品組合優化，且內部效率提升，稅後 EPS 16.55 元，優於預期 
亞德客-KY 2Q26 營收達 121.6 億元，+21.5%QoQ，+35.9%Y oY，係因電子產
業維持雙位數成長 、 汽車製造因持續增加市佔+20%Y oY， 其中電池產業在中國
補貼政策下需求持續強勁，照明終端需求仍不佳，不過衰退幅度已有收斂跡
象；稼動率部分：氣動元件產品維持 100-110%高檔水準，線性滑軌需求持續
改善，稼動率由 1Q26 之 30%上升至 40%。獲利方面，毛利率 49.7%，優於
預期，係因 1)線性滑軌產能利用率回升； 2)氣動元件進入復甦週期；營益率
35.1%，優於預期之 34.6%，業外損失 600 萬人民幣，其中 2700 萬為固定資
產處分損失(拆除舊廠房重建以提升效率 )，稅後 EPS 16.55 元，優於預期之
15.47 元。

3Q26 電子與汽車行業拉貨動能
強勁，調整稅後 EPS 至 14.48 元 
 3Q26 電子與汽車行業拉貨動能強勁，調整稅後 EPS 至 14.48 元 
展望後市，終端市場：電子產業受惠 AI 相關設備投資、客戶新產品推出及規
格升級，需求維持雙位數成長；汽車產業持續透過市占率提升帶動成長；電池
受惠 ESS 需求及中國廠商海外擴產，維持強勁復甦，管理層表示 7 月出貨維
持強勁，優於原 先預期。氣動元件持續擴增中國市占率，目前訂單能見度約
2-3 個月；線軌需求穩步改善，稼動率已提升至 40%以上，目標於 2026 年底
提升至 50%，總結前述，當前接單金額大於出貨金額，雖第三季受傳統淡季
影響營收將較第二季回落，但在去年基期偏低下，年增率仍可望維持強勁表
現。獲利部分，受惠生產效率持續提升、產品組合改善及氣動元件稼動率維持
高檔，毛利率可望維持穩健，在費用控管得宜，不考慮一次性業外損益下，調
整稅後 EPS 至 14.48 元(原 13.01 元)。

速，持續推升汽車/電池/太陽能等產業市佔率，費用控管得宜，不考慮一次性

EPS 為 71.65 元。

 產業谷底復甦，維持買進評等，目標價 1800 元 
管理層對中國氣動元件市場指引正向 ，觀察線軌需求逐步轉強，且日本工具機
訂單持續復甦，公司預計提升線軌稼動率表現以改善部門毛利率水準，評價部
分，當前公司評價處 21x，過往評價區間處 21-33x 水準，處中性下緣，考量產
業處谷底復甦，故維持買進評等，目標價 1800 元(25x2027EPS)。

MoM+2.09% 。預估亞德客 3Q26 營收金額 110.07 億元，

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 59.00 元 
亞德客 2026 年在對等關稅對中國大陸客戶在產能擴充決策 ， 雖然
會有遞延影響，但整體市場需求己有逐步回溫，加上亞德客持續

運持續成長。預估亞德客 2026 年營收金額 435.00 億元，
YoY+26.71% ，預估稅後盈餘 118.00 億元， YoY+40.47% ，預估
稅後 EPS 59.00 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 68.00 元 
亞德客 2027 年在中國政府十五五計畫強調智慧製造及產業升級
伴隨其他刺激政策，有助氣動元件市場需求，加上中國在地化採
購趨勢成型，有助亞德德市占率持續攀升。宏遠預估亞德客 2027
年營收金額 488 億元 ，YoY+12.19% ， 預估稅後盈餘136.00 億元 ，
YoY+15.25% ，預估稅後 EPS 68.00 元。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，資本額 20.00 億元，主要以生產氣動元件
電磁閥為主， 1995 年正式進軍中國大陸市場，並逐步將生產線移
住大陸，生產基地包括江蘇寧波、廣東佛山與台灣台南。亞德國
際集團產品主要以特殊規格氣缸為主，生產閥類產品包括電磁
閥、氣動閥、手動閥、手拉閥、機械閥與節流閥等十大類六十多
個系列數百個品種，為專業氣動元件廠商，並自有品牌「 AirTAC 」
行銷全世界。

圖一、公司組織架構

資料來源：亞德客、宏遠投顧整理

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
亞德客集團營收來源主要有氣動控制元件 29~33% 、氣動執行元
件 37~41% 、氣源處理元件 10~13% 與其他 16~20% 。下游終端應
用較分散，如電子設備 22~28% 、包裝機械 7~8% 、電池行業
8~14% 、汽車產業 6~7% 、通用機械 6~7% 、機床設備 5~7% 、能
源與照明 6~14% 、其他產業 25~28% 。在營收地區方面，中國大
陸地區比重 94%，非中國大陸地區比重 6%。在產業淡旺季方面，
2Q 旺季、 3Q 次旺季、 4Q 與 1Q 為傳統淡季。

客 2026 年營運成長重點包括： 1，在對等關稅對中國大陸客戶在
產能擴充決策，有遞延影響，整體市場 1Q26 需求己有逐步回溫，
加上中國大陸政府仍會視狀況，持續發布政策支持，有助氣動元
件需求回溫。 2，觀察各產業況，電池產業受惠於海外市場放寬中
國廠商限制，預期將帶動電池廠商成長 20~30% ；汽車產業方面，
雖然汽車產業未顯者復甦，亞德客將提升品牌形象，增加汽車產
業滲透率，推估汽車產業成長 10~15% ；電子產業方面，受惠電
子設備客戶推出更多新機型與客戶產品升級，將有助電子產業客
戶成長 10~15% 。3，亞 德 客-KY 不斷開發新產品 、 提升品牌形象，
並依各產業狀況提出相對應銷售策略提高市占率，提升客戶黏著
度。 4，線性滑軌在營運規模逐漸放大，預估貢獻亞德客 2026 年
營收金額 34~38 億元 ，YoY+36~46% 。 綜合上述 ， 預估亞德客2026
年營收金額 435 億元， YoY+26.71% 。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

華邦電 (2344 TT)  Buy

記憶體報價續漲，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：AI 的發展改變產業追求性價比與記憶體單價的傳統 ，
轉而重視效能、算力及大容量。記憶體市場供需出現結構性改
變。進入 3Q26 DRAM 與 Flash 的價格仍維持上行趨勢，雖然價
格上漲趨勢較緩，但毛利率仍持續向上。預估華邦電 2026 年稅
後 EPS 為 24.85 元。除 AI 伺服器需求外，交換器及 Edge AI 應
用亦帶動 NOR Flash 與 SLC NAND 需求持續成長，華邦電憑藉
NOR Flash 全球市占率第一及 SLC NAND 擴產，可望持續受惠。
此外，公司矽電容目前產能接近滿載，受惠 AI 先進封裝需求快
速成長，將成為新的獲利來源。過去 3~4 個月產業雖歷經多項
利空干擾，但 DRAM 及 NAND 報價持續走揚，顯示供需結構未
見轉弱，對華邦電建議 Buy ，目標價 200 元 (PER8X) 。  
華邦電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 5.40 元：受惠記憶體報價上漲， 2Q26 華
邦電合併營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% 。 CMS( 原  Specialty 
DRAM 改名客製化記憶體方案 )占營收比重  53% 、Flash 占營收
比重  33% 、 Logic 占  13% ，其中  CMS 營收 QoQ+78.21% ，成
長最明顯。 2Q26 合併毛利率為  66.25% ，增加約  12.88 個百分
點。2Q26 記憶體的毛利率從  1Q26 的  56.6% 提升至  70.3% ，
1Q26 稅後 EPS 5.40 元。  
資本支出 395 億元 ：受設備交機時程影響，預估 2026 年資本支
出下修為 395 億元， YoY+618.18% 。部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨
的設備將遞延跨年至 2027 年付款，但整體擴產進度不僅如期，
甚至超前，來自製程的轉換。目前台中廠  DRAM 月產能約  
12K 。配合高雄廠  16nm 新產能於 2027 年開出，公司規劃於  
2027 年將台中廠  DRAM 月產能降至約  7K 。高雄廠  Module 
A 目前月產能約  15K ，預計在  2026 年底前提升至 24K 並開
始新增產能投片。 16nm 月產能目前約  2K 。華邦電已正式啟動
高雄廠  Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標 2029 年年中開始
裝機，並於  2029 年底前進入量產。

2 群益投顧 
  華邦電 ESG ：  
華邦電堅持所有經營管理活動必須兼顧企業社會責任。經由加強員工教育
訓練及公司內控制度，以確保企業道德及政府法令之遵循。同時，持續不斷
地追求品質管理和環境保護之改善，勠力於社會公益，維持與利害關係人間
之良好溝通。期待所有事業夥伴支持並遵循企業社會責任之要求。

面向  內容

書

會

華邦電 2Q26 營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% ，稅後淨利 243.17 億元，
QoQ+140.42% ，稅後 EPS 5.40 元：  
受惠記憶體報價上漲， 2Q26 華邦電合併營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% 。
CMS( 原  Specialty DRAM 改名客製化記憶體方案 )占營收比重  53% 、 Flash
占營收比重  33% 、 Logic 占  13% ，其中  CMS 營收 QoQ+78.21% ，成長最
明顯。 2Q26 華邦電記憶體營收為  517.00 億元， QoQ+72.78% ， YoY 
+303.15% 。Flash 和  CMS 各占記憶體營收  39% 和  61% 。華邦電 2Q26 合
併毛利率為  66.25% ，較 1Q26 的 53.37% 增加約  12.88 個百分點。 2Q26 
記憶體的毛利率從  1Q26 的  56.6% 顯著提升至  70.3% ，產能利用率維持
100% 的滿載狀態，邏輯 (新唐 )毛利率則為  38.0% 。

3 群益投顧 
營業費用方面， 2Q26 合併營益率為  48.43% ，稅後獲利為  243.17 億元，
QoQ +140.42% ，稅後 EPS 5.40 元。每股淨值從  1Q26 的  25.92 元增加至
36.67 元。    
若單獨以記憶體來看， 2Q26 記憶體產品營收比重方面， Flash 產品線占記
憶體營收比重  39% ，營收 QoQ +65% 、YoY+176% ，位元出貨量 (bit shipment)
QoQ+14~+16% 、YoY+14~+16% ，在 NOR 與 SLC NAND 供給偏緊的態勢下，
成功推升 ASP (US$) QoQ+41~+43% 、 YoY>+100% 。 32nm SLC NAND Flash
占  Flash 營收比重為  25% ，24nm 占比達  4% ，製程持續轉進； 45nm Flash 
占比維持  6% 。高容量產品線整體出貨占比持續提升，反映 NOR 與 SLC 
NAND 需求暢旺，自 2Q26 起， 24nm SLC NAND 將逐步帶來顯著營收貢
獻。。  
CMS 占記憶體營收 61%，營收  QoQ +78% 、YoY +467% ，位元出貨量  QoQ 
-11~-13% 、YoY+21~+23% ，資料中心、 Edge AI 及智慧家庭等應用帶動結構
性供給缺口，使得 ASP (US$) QoQ 約 100% 、 YoY+>300% 。 2Q26 的  20nm 
占營收比重達  71% ，較  1Q26 的  68% 提升，主要受惠於 AI 強勁需求，
DDR4/LPDDR4 產品線的顯著成長與產品組合優化， ≦25nm 營收貢獻占比
達 93% 。  
從產品應用比重來看 ， 消費性電子  (Consumer) 占營收比重 28%，QoQ +64% ；
通訊電子  (Communication) 占營收比重  29% ， QoQ+82% ，受惠於網通與
WiFi 應用需求成長。電腦相關  (Computer) 占  15% ，QoQ+89% ，筆電需求
保持健康；車用及工業用相關  (Car & Industrial) 占營收比重  28% ， QoQ 
+54% ，車用需求穩定增加，使用容量增加，而工業和網路設備的  SLC 晶
片 design-in 數量也正在增加。相關應用對高密度記憶體與  Secure Flash 的
需求持續提升。

4 群益投顧 
395 億元， YoY+618.18% ，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%( 特別是晶圓廠
設備 )，以加速增產。主要因產能擴充及設備交貨延遲，部分設備交貨延至
2027 年。儘管目前許多機器設備交期拉長，部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨的設
備將遞延跨年至 2027 年付款，但整體擴產進度不僅如期，甚至超前，來自
製程的轉換。 1H26 已實際支出新台幣  75 億元。  
目前台中廠  DRAM 月產能約  12K 。配合高雄廠  16nm 新產能於  2027 年
開出，公司規劃於  2027 年將台中廠  DRAM 月產能降至約 7K。  
高雄廠  Module A 目前月產能約 15K ，預計在  2026 年底前提升至 24K 並
開始新增產能投片。 16nm 月產能目前約  2K ，現階段良率接近  80% ，目標
在  2026 年底前提升至接近 90% 。長期理想目標是將  16nm 產能拉高至
16K ，逐步取代舊製程。受惠於 16nm 產能的開出，預估  2027 年高雄廠的
位元產出將接近倍增。  
高雄廠  Module A 達到  24K 產能後，空間已塞滿，因此華邦電已正式啟動
高雄廠  Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標在  01/2027 正式動工，預計
2029 年年中開始裝機，並於  2029 年底前進入量產。 Module B 的潔淨室
面積約  30,000 平方公尺，約為現有  Module A 的兩倍。完整裝滿後可容納
約  50K 至  60K 的月產能，但公司強調會配合客戶的預測與  LTA( 長期合
約 )採分階段裝機，不會一次裝滿。  
規劃生產  Standard DRAM 、 CUBE DRAM( 客製化記憶體 )、 Wafer-on-
Wafer( 晶圓堆疊 )及矽電容 (Si-Cap) 。 Module B 已預留 14nm 、 12nm 及
EUV( 極紫外光 )的製程空間。其中  14nm 將同時開發非  EUV 及  EUV 版
本， EUV 版為後續  12nm 做準備。因  EUV 設備交期長達  3 至  3.5 年，
公司必須提前向設備廠規劃。已有客戶開始提前洽談  2029 至  2030 年
Module B 的產能與  LTA 。  
公司先前投入約新台幣  40 億元建立額外的矽電容產線，目前產能已全數
售罄。矽電容採用獨立產線配置，因此擴產與裝機完全不會排擠到  16nm
DRAM 的產能與設備規劃。由於產能供不應求，公司目前正積極在高雄廠
尋找新的空間以利後續擴產。值得注意的是，現階段規劃的  395 億元資本
支出尚未包含高雄廠新廠  Module B 的建置與裝機費用，未來的  Module B 
裝機將會是另一筆巨大的投資金額。

5 群益投顧 
圖一：華邦電記憶體資本支出

資料來源：華邦電，群益投顧預估整理 
華邦電在矽電容發展較預期佳：  
華邦電的矽電容起初並非獨立規劃的生意，而是華邦電在開發客製化記憶
體 (Cube) 時，為解決電壓驟降 (voltage drop) 而提供給客戶的保護電路。由於
產品規格極佳，部分非客製化記憶體的客戶進而主動要求試作，華邦電順勢
將其發展成一項獨立產品。  
隨著 AI、先進封裝 (如 CoWoS 等 )及細微穩壓 (1.2 伏、 0.9 伏以下 )的需求爆
發，矽電容因具備極小、極薄、耐高溫且電容值精準的特性，迅速成為解決
封裝雜訊與穩壓的最佳方案。  
華邦電矽電容的規格：目前正進行小量產的規格為  3,300 nF/mm ²，而最新
送樣的規格已達到  5,400 nF/mm ²。台積電的量產規格為 2,500 nF/mm ²(主
要用於其  interposer 上供客戶使用 )，其他同業則約在  1,500 nF/mm ²。華 邦
電在此領域具備顯著的領先優勢，並預期很快會成為全球最大的供應商。  
為避免矽電容生產擠壓到利潤更好的 DRAM 產能，華邦電特別為矽電容開
闢獨立的生產專線。公司先前已針對此專線投資約新台幣  40 億元，但產能
一開出便售光，目前高雄廠的矽電容產能已呈現滿載狀態，公司正積極尋找
新的空間進行擴產 。 由於矽電容的前景看好，目前已有數家原先製造  MLCC 
的廠商轉而進入此領域，並成為華邦電該項服務的客戶。  
相較於生產需要耗時 170 天的  DRAM ，矽電容的生產 cycle time 只需要 30
天。在相同的生產時間內， DRAM 跑 1 個生產 cycle ，矽電容則可以跑 5 個
cycle ，這大幅提升設備的周轉率。  
華邦電原本預期矽電容在  2027 年時能占  DRAM 營收的  10% 。然而由於
DRAM 本身的營收分母在近期成長得太大 ， 要達到  10% 的占比變得困難 ，
目前評估能維持  5% 營收占比。不過，矽電容發展的絕對總金額與公司原
先計畫的預期差不多，公司依舊看好其長期的成長動能。  
價格維持高檔，預估華邦電 2026 年營收 2,615.23 億元， YoY+192.51% ，稅
後獲利 1,118.29 億元， YoY+2,722.59% ，稅後 EPS 24.85 元：

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 7 日

6 群益投顧 
華邦電在 AI 邊緣運算方面，提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra Bandwidth
Element) 方案，並參加 UCIe 聯盟，華邦電自主開發高頻寬 CUBE 記憶體，
適用於邊緣 AI 應用， AI、 HPC 領域的  3D TSV DRAM ，華邦電目前 CUBE
在概念驗證 (POC) 階段，先在台中廠試做中，先以 25Snm 開發，後續會以
20nm/16nm 製程生產，可提供每顆晶片 256Mb~16Gb 容量， 2026 年有 16nm
製程， 2027 年可望貢獻營收， Hybrid bump WoW 可以推到 8 顆，再去切，
是以 good die base 出貨。華邦電之前與聯電 (2303 TT) 等合作夥伴成立晶圓
對晶圓 (wafer-to-wafer, W2W) 3D IC 專案，協助客戶加速 3D 封裝產品生
產。此項合作案聯電負責 CMOS 晶圓製造和晶圓對晶圓混合封裝技術；華
邦電導入客製化超高頻寬元件  (Customized Ultra- Bandwidth Elements, 
CUBE) 架構，用於邊緣運算 AI 設備，實現在各種平台和介面上的無縫部署，
但目前尚未出貨。  
管理層明確定位其角色更接近  ASIC ，配合客戶  XPU 進行  3D 封裝，使
華邦電與大宗  DRAM 商品市場的供給溢出效應脫鉤 。 一旦  CUBE 2027 年
起放量，公司的訂價邏輯將不再跟隨  DRAM 現貨市場起伏，而是依附在客
戶的  AI 晶片投資週期之上，這是一種更具黏性、更高毛利的客戶關係結
構。  
Code Storage Flash 方面， NOR Flash 供給在高容量終端需求帶動下持續偏
緊，推升價格於 1H26 起維持有利的上行趨勢。 SLC NAND 供給短缺態勢
亦升溫，支撐價格在  2026 年後延續上漲動能。主要成長來自  伺服器、車
用、 AI 相關應用。在消費端，隨  TWS 等應用功能複雜化，具備較小尺寸
優勢的  45nm Flash 持續推動  Design-in 。新產品自  2Q26 起， 24nm SLC 
NAND 將逐步帶來顯著的營收貢獻。    
Customized Memory Solution (CMS) 方面， DDR4 / LPDDR4 存在結構性供
給缺口，預期將延續至  2028 年以後，短期供給改善空間有限。在供給吃緊
及  AI 需求 (資料中心、 Edge AI 等 )挹注下， 2Q26 價格上漲空間明顯，對
2H26 至  2027 年維持正向展望。眾多客戶的系統單晶片 (SoC) 仍採用  22nm 
或  14nm 製程，短期內難以支援  DDR5 ，使  DDR4 需求得以延續  。隨著
三大原廠將製程升級至  14nm 以下，受限於  ECC 補償機制的技術差異，
已無法回頭量產  DDR4 。隨著大廠逐步淡出供給，現有產能已不足以滿足市
場長期的結構性需求，這正驅動  DDR4 與  DDR3 的合約價持續攀升，且
漲勢預期將一路延續至  2026 年底  。在應用端，智慧家庭、無人機、 M2M 
及網通設備在  2026 年的需求極為看好，電視相關應用需求亦維持健康。特
別是  20nm 製程在  2025 年呈現指數型成長，營收占比已達  55% ，這股成
長動能預計將持續貫穿整個  2026 年。  
過去簽  LTA 的主要是華邦電的直接客戶 (EC1) ，但 1Q26 管理層明確指出，
連二、三線終端廠商 (EC2、EC3) 乃至整車廠原廠，都開始繞過中間層直接找
上華邦電要求簽署長達三年的合約，部分  DDR4 客戶甚至提出 7-10 年的
超長期鎖貨需求。這種行為在供應鏈中屬於高度罕見，清楚說明市場對
DDR4 中長期供應的焦慮已從系統廠蔓延至整個生態鏈末端。對華邦電而
言， LTA 需求的激增提升營收能見度。  
Samsung 、 SK Hynix 、 Micron 陸續退出  2D NAND(SLC ／ MLC) 市場，將資
源集中於  3D NAND 高容量產品。這對華邦電而言，競爭者主動騰出的利
基空間，使其在  SLC NAND 的定價主導權顯著提升。 DRAM 端亦是相同

### Page 7

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 7 日

7 群益投顧 
邏輯：三大廠將  DDR4 產能陸續轉向  DDR5 及更先進製程，導致  DDR4 
供應持續吃緊，華邦電的中小容量  DDR4 產品 (4Gb 、 8Gb 、 16Gb) 在
Enterprise SSD Cache 領域幾乎面臨無競爭對手局面。  
華邦電表示，進入 3Q26 ， DRAM 與  Flash 的價格仍維持上行趨勢，但管
理階層表示不預期 ASP 會再次出現單季 100% 的翻倍增長。 CMS 的價格將
大致跟隨市場報價進行調整。由於全球主要供應商逐步淡出  2D NAND 及
低容量  eMMC( 迫使約 15% – 20% 的低容量需求轉向  SLC NAND) ，在供給
持續緊縮且客戶強勁的設計導入 (design-ins) 支持下 ， 價格漲勢預期將穩定延
續至  2H26 。 NOR Flash 受惠於 AI 伺服器，單台新世代機架估計需使用約
500 – 600 顆  NOR Flash 、車用等強勁需求，高容量  NOR Flash 市場持續
供不應求，將推升價格上行趨勢至  2026 年以後。由於華邦電在  NOR Flash 
市場穩居全球第一市占率 (約  23%) ，公司表示會兼顧市場份額與客戶關係，
不會主動帶頭大幅漲價。  
華邦電基本不參與波動較大的現貨市場，主要採行季度合約報價 (quarterly 
base) 。所有產品全數以合約價成交、不走現貨市場，顯示公司對後續定價走
勢具有相當程度的掌控力。由於管理階層預期 2027 年的記憶體供應將會比
2026 年更加吃緊。公司在滿足客戶基本合約需求後，選擇在 2Q26 適度調
控與減少出貨量，將部分產品留到後續再出貨，以因應未來幾年產能吃緊的
市況並發揮最大商業價值。預估華邦電 2026 年稅後 EPS 24.85 元。

11 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

智邦 (2345 TT)  Buy

AI 驅動算力與高速網路需求帶動成長，維持 Buy。  
投資建議 ：受惠於 AI 基礎建設持續擴張，帶動算力與高速網路
需求持續成長， 800G 乙太網路升級潮與北美 CSP 第三代運算
加速 ASIC 模組與伺服器持續為今年營運成長主力，維持 Buy
建議。  
進入營運旺季，帶動營收、獲利成長 ： 2Q26 ，雖然北美 CSP 客
戶 AI Server 進入新舊產品交換期，因進入傳統 Switch 營運旺
季，由於 AI 基礎建設建置算力需求未歇，加上 Agentic AI 對高
速網路頻寬需求大幅增加，推升 800G 乙太網路交換器出貨成
長，成為營運成長主軸。  
進入營運旺季，帶動營收、獲利成長： 展望 3Q26，北美電商 CSP
客戶 AI Server 進入第三代運算加速 ASIC 量產期，由於算力需
求持續上揚， AI 基礎建設持續擴張，推動 ASIC 加速器模組以
及 L10 伺服器出貨成長 ， 加上進入傳統 Switch 營運旺季 ，Agentic 
AI 對高速網路頻寬需求大幅增加，推升 800G 乙太網路交換器
出貨成長，持續推動營運向上。  
AI 算力基礎建設持續拉動營運成長： Agentic AI 的流行，持續
推升對高速網路頻寬的需求，帶動 800G 甚至 1.6T 乙太網路交
換器出貨成長，另一方面，由於北美 CSP 大客戶的 AI 模型客
戶在全球 AI Coding 市占率名列前茅，對算力需求孔急，以因
應快速擴張的 AI 業務，預期 2H26 量產的第三代 ASIC Server
將貢獻可觀營收，此外，由於該 AI 模型客戶營運快速增長，預
期對北美 CSP 客戶的第四代 ASIC Server 有更高的算力需求，
進一步推升未來的出貨量展望，此外， 2027 年的高速網路交換
器將過渡到 1.6T ，但因 Agentic AI 的需求成長，高速網路交換
器的需求將由資料中心擴及大企業，既有的 800G 交換器需求
不會消失，而是在既有的 800G 需求再疊加 1.6T 的出貨，故在
全球 AI 基礎建設持續擴張供應算力的趨勢下，將持續推升公司
營運成長。

5 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

順德(2351) 
Q2毛利率優預期，AI持續推升營運 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $252 
前次投資建議：2026.07.16 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $240

報告日期：2026.08.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元。 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片加入貢獻，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利再上層樓，EPS 8.4 元，
考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，上調投資評等為「買進」 ，目標價252 元(30X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 48.2%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/7 收盤價： 170.00 ➢ 展望 2026 年，預期AI 相關營收將倍數增長，車用受
惠庫存調整結束與新車款推出，均熱片預計認證後進度
可望加速。受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，
同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本業持續改善。綜上所
述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐 投
顧 上 修 順 德 2026 年營收  137.1 億 元 ，YoY 
+33.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 
億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十kW 往100 kW 
以上，過往 48V/54V 供電架構已不敷使用， 預期在
+-400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動AI導線架營
收持續高速增長；此外順德近兩年積極耕耘的散熱新事
業，預計在2027年將會開花結果，目前順德的均熱片
可做到約 30μm 的薄型化水準，優於同業約 50–60
μm，具備一定競爭優勢，預期在通過認證後產能陸續
開出，同時隨著CPU/GPU更大尺寸擴張，可望成為新
一輪成長主軸；車用則在電動化與在地化趨勢下維持穩
健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動率維持高檔下，毛利率
可望續揚，兆豐投顧預估順德 2027 年營收 154.8 億

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，但在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始出高功率導線架與穩壓器。而
順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散領域，並於 2026 年開始出 CPO Switch產
品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨。

個股報告 
車用持續復甦與 AI 導線架需求強勁，26Q2 營收與毛利率皆優預期，惟費
用增，EPS 1.6 元 
順德(2351)公告 26Q2 財報：營收 35.5 億元，QoQ +26.4%/YoY +36.6%，，
優於預期，主要受惠車用與消費性需求同步回溫、AI 導線架需求高速增長，
全產品線均季增超過 20%；同時在報價調漲與稼動率提升、且生產效率提升
下，毛利率 20.8%，季增1.7ppt，創近年新高，也優於研究部預估之 18.5%，
不過因人事獎金與一次性 RBA 移工費用(約 5,000 萬元)提列，使費用季增
21%至 3.2 億元，故稅後淨利 3 億元，QoQ +36.8%/YoY 虧為盈，EPS 1.6
元，略低於前次上修後之 1.7 元。

車用展望佳、AI 放量加速，上修獲利預估，26Q3 EPS 1.8 元 
順德(2351)公告 7 月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM +9.4%/YoY +60.8% ，再
創單月新高，展望 26Q3，兆豐投顧上修營收至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY 
+43.6%，主要動能來自車用需求全面復甦與  AI 導線架供不應求，車用方
面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將延續，
此外 AI 導線於 Q2 有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將逐月創高，
而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，預估毛利率維持 20%高檔水準，同步上
修稅後淨利 3.5 億元，QoQ +15.4% /YoY + 301%，EPS 1.8 元(原估 1.75
元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 40 支產品，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年 AI 相關
營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求激增與
新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩年，近
期通過 2 CSP 客戶廠房稽核，預計認證後進度可望加速，更大尺寸產品用於 
CPU/GPU 將陸續驗證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限。毛利率方面：
受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本
業持續改善。綜上所述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐投顧
上修順德 2026 年營收 137.1 億元，YoY +3 3.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 
7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
AI 產品全面放量，均熱片加入貢獻，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利再上層樓，
EPS 8.4 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，而順德
過去幾年與歐美大廠合作開發高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為
主要受惠者，預期在+-400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動 AI 導線架營
收持續高速增長；此外順德近兩年積極耕耘的散熱新事業，預計在 2027 年
將會開花結果，目前順德的均熱片可做到約 30μm 的薄型化水準，優於同業
約 50–60μm，具備一定競爭優勢，預期在通過認證後產能陸續開出，同時
隨著 CPU/GPU 更大尺寸擴張，可望成為新一輪成長主軸；車用則在電動化
與在地化趨勢下維持穩健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動率維持高檔下，毛利
率可望續揚，兆豐投顧預估順德 2027 年營收 154.8 億元，YoY +12.9%，
毛利率 21.3%，年增 1ppt，稅後淨利 16.3 億元，YoY +30%，EPS 8.4 元，
營運再創新高。

### Page 6

Wiwynn (6669.TW)
10 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Wiwynn
Valuation
Our target price for Wiwynn is NT$9,500. We value Wiwynn based on PE as we believe the company’s earnings performance
and industry dynamics will be the key focus areas for the market. Our TP is based on 18x 2027E EPS, which is at the top end of
the historical trading range since listing and above the average PE multiple of the peer group. We believe our target multiple is
justified by the company’s 100% exposure to data centers, rising scale, and robust earnings growth, as well as stabilizing ROE.

Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Wiwynn’s shares to trade below our target price include: 1) Potential volatility in capex
deployment; 2) Component price hike; 3) Peer competition; and 4) FX volatility.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

Wiwynn Corp  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 OpM in line with
expectations; stay OW
More details:
• 2Q GM was 9.3% (+200bps q/q), 120/90bps above MSe/Street, which we
attribute to a higher consignment model mix in the quarter.
• 2Q opex was higher, primarily from higher R&D expenses related to
upcoming new projects (+99% q/q), so OP came in at NT$20.2bn (+23% q/q,
+69% y/y), 7% below MSe but 2% above consensus. OpM is 7.3% (+160bps
q/q), while showing 70bps beat vs. our prior estimate and 30bps above the

Street, but we view it as in line because Wistron had already reported a few
days earlier and we were able to back out Wiwynn's OpM at ~7.3% already,
which we mentioned in our Wistron report here.
• Non-op losses also higher than expected, mainly owing to higher interest
expenses.
• Thus, NP came in at NT$15bn (+9% q/q, +53% y/y), for EPS of NT$80.55, 7%
below MSe but in line with the Street.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+

Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

Taiwan Electronics & Semiconductors
11 August 2026 Citi Research
2
ODMs and PC Supply chain - For NB ODMs, overall NB shipments in July declined
31% MoM as end demand has been dampened by the increasing retail prices due to
components cost hikes and the high base effect of Jun at the quarter end. We now
see downstream NB ODMs becoming more conservative over the unit shipment
outlook in 2H. Given the early pull-in and the increasing constraint in components

procurement, we expect 3Q26E NB shipments to decline -8% QoQ/-18% YoY. While
the price hike effect in the NB products should partially offset shipment decline, we
expect increasing margins dilution impact given the Buy and Sell model for NB.
Component and power  - Jul-26 sales as reported by thermal players (AVC & Auras)
were up from Jun at +10% MoM/+65% YoY. We believe the revenue momentum is
resuming after the GPU platform transition in 2Q26. We expect both companies to

see sequential revenue growth in 3Q26E, driven by the ramp-up of the AI ASIC
platform and the incremental order of GB300. Vera Rubin's contribution is expected
to begin at the end of 3Q26. On power supply, Delta’s July sales were NT$67bn (up
2% MoM and 48% YoY), in line with market expectations. With higher power
consumption for VR NVL72, even 800V adoption is not mandatory, and we see value
upgrade trend to benefit Delta on better revenue growth trend ahead.

For passive components - Yageo reported continuously trending up monthly sales
in Jul-26, ahead of mgmt. ’s guidance for the current quarter. This was driven by
demand for AI-related applications remaining strong, and both standard and
specialty products continuing to show steady growth. The broad-based business
momentum and the elevated BB ratio at 2.2 could support continued revenue
growth into 3Q26E.
BBU  —  Jul-26 sales data from Dynapack and AES provide further evidence that

BBU demand remains robust. Dynapack’s group sales reached NT$1.2bn, up 21% y/y
despite a modest 3% m/m decline due to weaker low margin IT demand. More
importantly, we estimate its BBU sales tripled m/m to c.NT$600mn as shipment of
a previously delayed BBU product resumed from mid-July. We expect this ramp to
continue, driving solid top-line momentum of the company. Meanwhile, AES
maintained strong sales momentum at a high level in July-26 +28% y/y and +2%

m/m, following its robust 2Q performance, indicating that healthy demand remains.
Taken together, the July-26 sales strengthen our positive investment thesis on BBU
sector – AIDC BBU adoption continues to accelerate higher power products are
supporting both sales growth and margins, and expanding customer programs
should provide solid earnings visibility into 2H26 and 2027. Dynapack remains our
preferred BBU play given its more pronounced growth inflection and earnings

upside.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

activities and services intended to benefit the investor clients of Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and its affiliates (the “Firm”).
Compensation is not linked to specific transactions or recommendations. Like all Firm employees, analysts receive
compensation that is impacted by overall Firm profitability which includes investment banking, sales and trading, and

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.
For financial instruments recommended in the Product in which the Firm is not a market maker, the Firm is a liquidity provider

recommended in the Product. The Firm regularly trades in the securities of the issuer(s) discussed in the Product. The Firm may

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-10
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide updates after Chenbro’s 2Q26 results and 
downgrade our rating of its stock to Outperform (2) from Buy (1).

What’s the impact: Business outlook. While we do not expect a major 
change in AI server shipment forecasts in the near term, Chenbro will face 
short-term hiccups from 3Q26 as one of its key clients is suffering from a 
memory shortage. This might, in our view, trigger weaker-than-our-
originally-expected revenue momentum from 3Q26. In the long term, 
Chenbro highlights its new business strategy to proliferate its product 
offering from chassis-level to rack-level, which is why it is expanding to a 
new factory (leasing with total equipment capex of TWD600m) to support 
future growth. Although we believe some of the customised racks will keep 
a c.30% gross margin in the short term, we expect that to decline to c.25% 
when Chenbro grows its rack revenue to a certain size. Overall, we believe 
Chenbro might not only have to face short-term headwinds from its key 
client, but also encounter gross-margin dilution with its rising addressable 
market in the long term. Though we appreciate Chenbro’s effort to expand 
its total addressable market, it does not involve meaningful spec upgrades 
compared with its AI peers, which might lead to a de-rating of Chenbro’s 
PER multiple. Nonetheless, we believe its fundamentals are largely on 
track in our forecast period despite some short-term slowdown.

2Q26 results review: Chenbro’s 2Q26 earnings came in at TWD1.38bn 
(EPS: TWD11.03), which was 10% below our expectation but largely on 
track with the Bloomberg consensus. Chenbro’s 2Q26 revenue came in at 
TWD7.8bn (+10% QoQ and +44% YoY). Although the number was largely 
on track with the consensus, we believe our overestimation of the HGX and 
ASIC AI server chassis contributions in 2Q26 was the key reason for the 
7% gap between our estimate and the actual number. Gross and operating 
margins came in largely on track vs. consensus, with strength due to 
ongoing NRE recognition. Lastly, net income was impacted by a higher tax 
rate in 2Q26 (26.3% vs. 23.8% in 1Q26).

What we recommend: We cut our 2026-28E EPS by 3-10% to factor in a 
weaker revenue momentum in the short term. We downgrade our rating of 
Chenbro to Outperform (2) from Buy (1) and cut the 12M TP to TWD1,235, 
based on a lower target PER multiple of 23x (from 34x), applied to our 1-
year-forward EPS forecast, due to short-term hiccups and less spec 
upgrade vs. peers. Key downside risk: worse-than-expected AI server 
demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are lower than the Bloomberg 
consensus by 5-12% due mainly to our more bearish view on profitability.

10 August 2026

Downgrading: short-term hiccups

➢ Short-term demand negatively impacted by component shortage  
➢ Will enjoy less spec upgrade than its AI component peers long-term  
➢ Downgrading to Outperform (2); lowering 12M TP to TWD1,235

Company profile 
Founded in 1983 and listed on the Taiwan stock exchange in 2011, Chenbro started as the first 
computer chassis manufacturer in Asia with its own-brand rackmount systems, tower servers and PC 
chassis. In 3Q25, server-related products accounted for 99% of revenue and PC chassis 1%. The 
company is headquartered in New Taipei City and owns production sites in Shulin, Chiayi, Kunshan and 
Dongguan. Meanwhile, Chenbro also owns service centres in the US, Germany, and China.

### Page 1

Summary: Microloops released its 2Q26 results on 11 August after market hours. Overall, 
its 2Q26 operating profit of TWD112m (+100.9% QoQ, +1.9% YoY) was largely in line with 
our and the consensus estimates. Likely with some FX losses, its 2Q26 EPS came in at 
TWD1, missing our and the consensus estimates of TWD1.09.

We have a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD1,325, based on a target PER of 40x 
applied to our one-year-forward EPS forecast. For more information on the company, please 
refer to our latest report, Positive outlook over 2026-28, published on 28 July 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 2Q26 results: core business largely in line with estimates. Microloops’ 2Q26 
revenue of TWD1,047m (+22.5% QoQ, +0.6% YoY) was in line with our and the 
Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 21.5% (vs. 18.2% in 
1Q26 and 20.1% in 2Q25), slightly lower than our (22.0%) and the consensus (22.5%) 
estimates. Its operating margin of 10.7% (vs. 6.5% in 1Q26 and 10.6% in 2Q25), was 
also slightly lower than our (10.9%) and the consensus (11.3%) estimates. Thus, its 
operating profit of TWD112m (+100.9% QoQ, +1.9% YoY) was roughly in line with our 
and the consensus estimates. Likely with some FX losses, its net profit of TWD68m 
(+51.5% QoQ, +166.2% YoY) missed our and the consensus estimates by 8.5% and 
8.1%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS finished at TWD1, missing our and the consensus 
estimates of TWD1.09.

• Intact revenue outlook over 2026-28E - at least double YoY. Due to likely rising 
liquid-cooling penetration over 2026-28 in ASIC and NIC cards, management maintains 
its revenue guidance of at least doubling YoY over 2026-28. Besides, amid a rising level 
of automation and improving production procedures, management expects the 
company’s gross and operating margins to improve QoQ in 2H26. Management also 
expects the revenue momentum to pick up MoM in 2H26, translating into record-high 
monthly revenue from July to December 2026. In 4Q26, the revenue drivers will include 
ASICs and CPUs, in our view. Microloops aims for a new CSP client to start contributing 
revenue in 2Q27, with another new CSP client expected to come on board in 2028.

What we recommend

applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Based on our 2026/27E EPS, the stock is

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
2Q26 Beat Reinforce AI Thermal Ramp; Raised TP to NT$3,800
CITI'S TAKE 
AVC delivered a solid 2Q26 beat, with reported net profit of NT$9.6bn 
topping Citi/cons estimates by 11%/12% respectively, driven by margin 
outperformance and non-ops boost. GPM improvement was above 
expectations on continued mix shift toward server and liquid cooling 
solutions. Mgmt. ’s guidance implies sequential growth led by AI ASIC ramp-
up, with Vera Rubin generation platform contribution expected by quarter-
end. We model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 3Q26E, accelerating further 
into 4Q26E as GPU and AI ASIC liquid cooling ramps. Reaffirmed 
2026E/27E capex also underscores confidence in capacity expansion, in our 
view. We raise our 2026-28E earnings by 5%/12%/18% to reflect updated 
guidance and constructive 2H26E outlook and lift our TP to NT$3,800 from 
NT$3,450 (25x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
2Q26 results beat on margins & non-ops — AVC’s reported 2Q26 net profit of 
NT$9.6bn (+21% QoQ/+139% Y o Y) beat Citi/cons forecast by 11%/12%, driven by 
better-than-expected margins and non-ops. 2Q26 GPM came in at 32.6% (+2.8ppts 
QoQ/+8.2ppts Y o Y), above both Citi/cons estimates, mainly due to further mix 
improvement and a higher server revenue contribution. Non-ops included NT$255m of 
net interest income, providing an incremental boost to the bottom.
Broadening AI ASIC ramp and GPU transition support 2H26E acceleration — 
Mgmt. guided for revenue growth in 2H26 compared to 1H26, primarily driven by 
mass production ramp-up of AI ASIC project, with the Vera Rubin generation 
platform expected to see contribution by quarter-end. We now model 16% QoQ 
revenue growth for 3Q26E, and expect further acceleration in 4Q26E as GPU and AI 
ASIC liquid cooling products ramp simultaneously. GPM guidance also points to 
sequential improvement vs. 2Q26, driven by a higher server/liquid cooling mix and 
stronger operating leverage. Mgmt. reaffirmed 2026E/27E capex guidance of 
NT$15bn/15+bn, reiterating confidence in ongoing capacity expansion.
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 5%/12%/18% to 
reflect updated guidance and constructive 2H26E outlook. We lift our TP to NT$3,800, 
still based on 25x 2027E EPS. We believe AVC’s solid supply position across key 
customers’ key AI GPU and AI ASIC projects, combined with a sustained revenue and 
margin growth trajectory, could continue to support the stock price strength. With the 
platform transition risk now appearing largely resolved and 2H26E visibility improving, 
we see continued upside to consensus estimates. We reiterate our Buy rating.

### Page 6

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible postion as Blackwell
and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider share of the
thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU opportunities,
providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$3,800 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Key highlights of the 2Q26 earnings call
2Q result beat on better OPM
Hon Hai delivered another record quarter, with 2Q26 revenue of NT$2.53tn (up
19% QoQ, 41% YoY) and OPM at 3.75% (vs 3.6% in 1Q26, beat consensus of 3.3%).
Management expects YoY OPM expansion for the year while cloud/networking
exceeded 50% of revenue for the first time in 2Q26. 3Q revenue is guided to grow
significantly QoQ/YoY, driven by continued AI growth plus the traditional peak

season.
Figure 1. Hon Hai - 2Q26 Results Overview
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus
AI Rack the Key Incremental Driver
Smart Consumer Electronics should grow significantly QoQ and YoY, while
Computing Products are expected to decline slightly QoQ as the new-product peak
passes and memory supply remains tight, but still grow significantly YoY.

Components & Others should also post significant YoY growth. Overall, AI Rack’s
will be the key incremental driver and management expects 3Q AI rack shipments
to grow high-double-digits QoQ as existing platforms continue ramping and next-
generation products begin production.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

Figure 4. Hon Hai - 2026E Business Outlook
Source: Company Reports
Networking Becomes the Next AI Growth Engine
Hon Hai sees networking and optical connectivity as the next major growth pillar after
AI compute. Networking revenue delivered strong growth in 1H26, driven by rising AI
cluster scale and continued upgrades in switch bandwidth. Its higher-end switch
products remain on schedule, with new-generation switches already shipping primarily

for scale-out connectivity between AI racks, while the product roadmap should expand
alongside future system architectures. More importantly, Hon Hai is moving beyond
switch assembly into a vertically integrated networking platform encompassing
optical transceivers, cables, connectors, high-speed interconnects, power modules
and advanced packaging. Management believes optical communications could
eventually represent a US$100bn+ addressable market, making networking an

increasingly meaningful contributor to both revenue and profitability. As AI clusters
become larger and more interconnected, the networking content per AI system should
rise structurally faster than server unit growth.
ASIC Expands Rapidly — Revenue Understates the Real
Business Scale
ASIC AI servers are emerging as a second major growth engine alongside Hon Hai’s
dominant GPU franchise. Management said the company has secured new ASIC
customers and projects in 2026, and expects its ASIC market share to increase

significantly, with a longer-term target of 40%+. Hon Hai’s differentiation is its
ability to participate across multiple layers—from modules and cooling systems to
Compute Trays, rack integration and complete system architecture, particularly for
increasingly complex liquid-cooled and high-power ASIC systems. ASIC
represented roughly 10% of AI-server revenue in 2025, with the 1H26
contribution slightly higher.  With new products ramping in 2H26, Hon Hai

expects both ASIC shipment scale and profit contribution to continue increasing.

### Page 6

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Figure 5. Hon Hai - AI Server Business Outlook
Source: Company Reports
Consignment vs. Buy-and-Sell: Lower Revenue, Better
Capital Efficiency
Compared to GPU-based project, ASIC projects currently have a significantly
higher consignment mix because system architecture and customer requirements
are more customized. Management expects consignment penetration to gradually
rise as ASIC projects expand, and GPU projects could also adopt consignment

depending on customer requirements. Financially, this reduces inventory exposure,
component-price risk and working-capital requirements while improving asset
turnover. With potentially increasing consignment business model thanks to rising
ASIC exposure and better OPM sustainability, we are positive on Hon Hai’s
earnings growth trajectory.
Capacity Expansion Accelerates Ahead of the Next AI Ramp
Hon Hai is aggressively expanding capacity ahead of the next AI product cycle.

Following capex of approximately NT$136.3bn in 2024 and NT$173.8bn in 2025,
management expects 2026 capex to increase by more than 30% YoY, primarily to
expand server, AI rack, liquid-cooling and testing capacity while strengthening
global manufacturing and automation. Expansion is underway across Taiwan, the
U.S., Mexico and Vietnam, with U.S. R&D/manufacturing capacity specifically
increasing in Texas, Wisconsin and California to support localization requirements.

Although rapid growth pushed 1H26 free cash flow to a NT$150bn outflow due to
higher working capital and NT$80.9bn of capex, underlying cash generation
remains strong: adjusted EBITDA increased 52% YoY, or NT$77.6bn, significantly
exceeding the increase in capex and interest expense. Management therefore
views current investment as growth-driven rather than defensive—the

### Page 7

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
7
combination of higher CSP capex, rising Hon Hai market share, vertical integration
and automation should translate the capacity build-out into stronger revenue,
profitability and capital efficiency over the coming years.
Reiterate Buy with TP NT$400
Thanks to better order visibility, robust OPM trend and more value upgrade for
next-generation AI infrastructure, we lift our 2026/2027 /2028 earnings estimates

by 8%/3%/8% for Hon Hai reflecting better order visibility and its advantage of
operational scale, vertical integration, automation and efficiency. We move our
target from 2026 to now 2026/2027 average, based on 18x PER, from prior 20x,
reflecting recent market concern on macro uncertainties.

Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)

10
Hon Hai Precision
Company description
Established in 1974, Hon Hai has become the largest EMS player in the world
in terms of sales. Through its vertical integration model or "eCMMS" (e-
enabled Components, Modules, Moves and Services), it provides
components, modules and system assembly products to many global OEMs
in the PC, communication and consumer sectors.
Investment strategy
We rate Hon Hai shares as Buy as we believe the company’s margins will

continue to improve, backed by a renewed focus on profitability, operational
efficiency and new businesses development. We think there’s more upside to
go from current level, as the underlying drivers are not just about product
cycle, but also about fundamental transformation and new business
opportunities.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is
at the higher end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is

justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook,
as well as new endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon
Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe
a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Jentech announced its 2Q26 results on 12 August 2026 
following market hours. We see Jentech as a leading global provider of 
thermal products for GPU projects, benefitting from the trend of rising TDP. 
We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, July 2026 revenue and 
June unaudited results ahead of estimates, 7 August 2026)

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. Jentech’s 2Q26 revenue was 
TWD7,275m (+37.1% QoQ and +40.4% YoY), beating our estimate and the 
Bloomberg consensus by 2.1% and 5.2%, respectively. Its 2Q26 gross 
margin came in at 46.8% (vs. 41.8% in 1Q26 and 41.5% in 2Q25), a 
record-high level, above our estimate (45.0%) and the consensus (44.3%), 
which is mainly due to better product mix, ie, heat spreaders for Rubin. Its 
operating profit was TWD2,749m (+62.0% QoQ and +61.8% YoY), beating 
our and consensus estimates by 4.9% and 12.5%, respectively, and thus its 
operating margin of 37.8% (vs. 32.0% in 1Q26 and 32.8% in 2Q25), a 
record-high level, was also higher than our estimate (36.8%) and the 
consensus (35.3%). With some financial asset evaluation gains, its net 
profit was TWD2,318m (+65.8% QoQ and +106.8% YoY), which beat our 
estimate and the consensus by 9.3% and 17.9%, respectively. Its 2Q26 
EPS came in at TWD15.80. Note its product mix for 2Q26 included heat 
spreaders (75.1%, +40.1% YoY), lead frames (8.6%, +33.2% YoY), 
electronic components (5.8%, +17.2% YoY), communication (0.9%, 
+103.6% YoY) and others (9.6%, +66.3% YoY).

Strong growth momentum should continue over 2H26-28E. Due to a 
brighter outlook for one-piece lids for Rubin, we now look for its 3Q26 
revenue to grow by 27.3% QoQ vs. Bloomberg consensus of 9.1% QoQ. 
The removable lids are expected to start shipping from 4Q26, with an ASP 
of >USD200. We lower our 2026E EPS by 5%, mainly because we factor in 
the expense from restricted stock in 3Q26 (EPS of c.TWD8 impact) but 
raise our 2027-28E EPS by 3-4%, mainly on our more aggressive gross 
margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 
12M TP of TWD6,770, still based on a target PER of 73x (vs. its past-3-
year PER range of 17-62x) applied to our 1-year forward EPS forecast. We 
believe our target valuation is fair, given Jentech’s strong earnings visibility 
and leading position in chip-level thermal solutions, which we believe 
warrant a re-rating. Downside risks: weaker-than-expected global AI server 
demand; slower-than-expected thermal spec upgrades.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 9-13% above consensus, likely as 
we are more aggressive in our revenue and gross margin assumptions.

12 August 2026

2Q26 review: improving profitability

➢ 2Q26 results: record-high gross and operating margins beat estimates 
➢ Promising spec upgrade story; intact leading position  
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call and 12-month TP of TWD6,770

Company profile 
Established in 1987, Jentech Precision Industrial is a leading global thermal-solutions and precision-
metal components maker that serves leading semiconductor and server vendors. As of 2025, its 
revenue comprised thermal heat spreaders (73%), lead frames (9%), electronic components (7%), 
communication connectors (1%) and others (10%).

### Page 1

Hon Hai Precision Industry

Buy: 2Q delivers a beat with solid margins; 
strong AI server outlook; lift PO to NT$320

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 320.00 TWD | Price: 270.00 TWD

2Q26 beat: larger scale and better margins driven by AI 
Hon Hai (HH)’s 2Q OP (+11% QoQ, +104% YoY) was 33%/18% above BofAe/consensus, 
supported by larger sales scale (7%/5% above BofAe/consensus) and a favorable mix. 
Cloud and networking accounted for 51% of 2Q26 sales, vs. 48%/41% in 1Q26/2Q25. 
Thus, GM/OPM of 6.12%/3.75% came in above BofAe of 6.08%/3.03% and consensus of 
6.05%/3.34%. 2Q26 OPM came in above HH’s target of 3.20%+. With a small non-op. 
gain of NT$63mn, NP (+33% QoQ, +42% YoY) was 13%/4% above BofAe/consensus.  
Server business as a key bright spot at 2Q26 earnings call 
Mgmt. guided 3Q26 sales will rise 4-15% QoQ (BofAe: +11% QoQ, +36% YoY), mainly 
led by cloud and networking business. Mgmt. is positive on the AI server outlook in 2026 
and expects rapid sales growth from cloud and networking business. Mgmt. guided AI 
server rack shipments will grow high-double-digit% QoQ in 3Q26 and kept the full-year 
growth target of more-than-doubled YoY unchanged. Mgmt. expects the Nvidia GB300 
product momentum to last till 2027 and Vera Rubin to start shipment from 4Q26. For 
ASIC (mainly under consignment business model), HH expects rising shipment and share 
from 2H26. Mgmt. targets 50%+ share in AI server market, vs. 40%+ now, and expects 
sales from general/CPU server to grow QoQ in 3Q/4Q26 with above industry levels. 
Buy: accelerating sales/earnings led by AI; new PO NT$320  
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 1-4%, mainly to reflect the latest guidance and higher 
sales driven by AI server. We raise our PO to NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS) from 
NT$305 (15x 2H26-1H27E EPS), with the valuation base rollover but the multiple 
remains unchanged. We reiterate Buy on Hon Hai in view of solid sales/earnings growth, 
driven by robust AI server momentum, and the company’s execution in sustaining OPM 
(despite the market’s ongoing concerns about margin dilution from AI server rack 
business), thanks to rising sales scale and ongoing component value growth.

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Hon Hai is the world's largest EMS provider, with four key 
business segments - Smart Consumer Electronics, Cloud & 
Networking, Computing, and Components & Others. Hon 
Hai supplies components, final assembly, and global 
logistics for global tech giants, including Apple, Microsoft, 
Google, HP, Dell, Cisco, and Sony. Hon Hai has 
R&D/production sites globally, including Taiwan, China, 
India, Japan, Vietnam, Malaysia, Czech Republic, Mexico, and 
the US. The company was established in Taiwan in 1974.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Hon Hai as its gradual moves into 
high margin/value-added server/components/semi 
business, stronger position in AI server with rising sales, 
solid balance sheet, and attractive valuation among Greater 
China AI server players.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Hon Hai Precision Industry (HNHAF) 
We set our PO at NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS), at around 3SD above historical P/E 
average since 2007. We think the valuation is justified as HH's leading position in 
EMS/ODM business, improving iPhone momentum with replacement cycle, rising sales 
from AI business, more diversified global footprint than peers to mitigate 
tariff/geopolitical risk, and improving balance sheet with higher cash dividend payout.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker momentum from Apple business (iPhone, iPad 
and Apple Watch), (2) increasing competition in EMS business from peers, (3) slower 
sales/earnings delivery in auto/EV business, and (4) weaker market demand or lower 
shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better-than-expected yield rate and demand for iPhone, 
iPad and Apple Watch business, (2) further automation adoption to reduce labor costs 
and improve efficiency, (3) faster sales/earnings delivery from auto/EV business, and (4) 
stronger market demand or higher shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

### Page 1

Nien Made Enterprise

2Q missed on one-offs but sales growth 
regained momentum, cut PO to NT$425

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 425.00 TWD | Price: 391.00 TWD

2Q EPS NT$4.75 missed on higher fuel surcharges and tax 
Nien Made reported 2Q revenue of NT$7.9bn (+2.9% YoY, or +0.5% YoY in USD terms), 
in line with previously reported monthly sales. By region, Europe (mid-20s% of sales) 
was the key growth driver, with revenue rising 16.9% YoY, while demand in the US 
remained soft amid a weaker macro backdrop. Gross margin reached a record high of 
60%, up 0.8ppt YoY and 0.9ppt above our forecast, driven by a more favorable product 
and geographic mix. However, operating profit came in 2% below our estimate at 
NT$2.4bn (-9.8% YoY), mainly due to higher fuel surcharges following the Iran-US 
conflict. Below the operating line, FX losses narrowed significantly to NT$100mn from 
NT$890mn in 2Q, but still surprised on the downside as we had assumed no FX loss for 
the quarter. In addition, a one-off retrospective tax payment in China lifted the effective 
tax rate to 32% from 27%, reducing EPS by roughly NT$0.3. As a result, net profit came 
in at NT$1.39bn, up 20.7% YoY but 16% below our forecast, with the miss largely 
attributable to one-off items. 1H26 net profit increased 3.7% YoY to NT$2.9bn. 
June and July regained growth supported by macro 
Sales growth reaccelerated in June and July, with sales increasing 11.7% and 16.4% YoY 
(MSD% growth in USD terms), vs. flat YoY in May. We attribute this partly to a recovery 
of demand in the US: according to the BAC credit and debit card spending data, US 
window coverings spendings’ decline narrowed to -3%/-4% in July/June, vs. -7% in 5M26 
(Exhibit 1).   
Cut PO to NT$425 on lower estimates; Reit. Buy  
We lower our FY26 earnings forecast by 11% to reflect the 2Q earnings miss and, 
accordingly, reduce our PO by 11% to NT$425, based on an unchanged 19x FY26 P/E. 
We reiterate our Buy rating. We reiterate our Buy rating as we believe the share-gain 
story and operating margins remains solid supported by improving Europe mix and 
custom-made mix.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
Interior Furnishings

Company Description 
Nien Made, founded in 1974, is one of the world's largest 
window-covering manufacturers. It has roughly 50% 
market share in both ready-made (OEM business) shades 
and blinds, and custom-made (own brand) shutters in the 
US. The US market accounted for over 75% of sales. Key 
clients include Home Depot and Wal-Mart for ready-made 
shades and blinds. Nien Made operates its own retail 
network and e-commerce business for its custom-made 
shutters under the Norman brand name in the US and 
Europe.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on Nien Made, mainly given 1) clear 
custom-made growth profile (share gain in the US and fast-
growing DTC in Japan and Europe), 2) its largest customer 
Home Depot is gaining market share, and 3) margin 
expansion potentials backed by favorable products mix.

Lotes Co. Ltd  | Asia Pacific
2Q GM missed on higher raw
material costs
2Q results:
• GM came in at 45.5% (-410bps q/q, -550bps y/y) 340bps below MSe and
380bps below the Street. This is mainly due to rising raw material costs and
depletion of low cost raw material inventory, with Lotes unable to raise
prices to end customers, until new project price negotiations.
• OPEX is  also higher than expected at NT$2.25B (+18% q/q), 17% above MSe

and 12% above the Street.
• So OpM declined to 22.0% (-710bps q/q, -920bps y/y), 790bps below MSe
and 740bps below the Street.
• Net profit was NT$1,984M (-17% q/q, +165% y/y) for EPS of NT$17.69, 11%
below MSe and 14% below the Street.
• Lotes is holding an earnings call on August 13 at 10am HKT; we will share
takeaways after the call.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lotes Co. Ltd. (3533.TW)
Primary - Residual Income
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.6% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected growth in the server business, such as from cloud and enter-
prise in China
n Stronger-than-expected DT demand

n Sooner-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n Faster ramp-up of its EV business
Risks to Downside
n Further deceleration of server demand from US hyperscalers
n Weaker-than-expected DT demand
n Slower-than-expected inventory digestion of NB
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Earnings revision: We factor in the 2Q26 earnings and revise up 2026E earnings by 1% 
on higher-than-expected GM and lower-than-expected Opex ratio, while non-OP is 
lower than expected. We keep 2027/ 28E earnings largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 18.9x is derived from a peers’ average ratio of 
trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY 
and OPM). Our target P/E of 18.9x is in the range of company’s historical forward P/E 
since 2010, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the 
competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
